TRN_country_collapse_decisions = {
	TRM_issuance_of_laws_for_local_humanity = {
		icon = GFX_decision_eng_trade_unions_support
		
		allowed = {
			original_tag = TRM
		}
		
		visible = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = TRN_country_collapse_decisions_end_flag }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = TRM_issuance_of_laws_for_local_humanity_flag }
		}
		
		fire_only_once = yes
		
		cost = 150

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0
		}

		complete_effect = {
			add_stability = -0.10
			add_to_variable = {
				global.NSD_country_collapse_decisions_value = -10
				tooltip = NSD_country_collapse_value_tt
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				global.KPD_country_collapse_decisions_value = -5
				tooltip = KPD_country_collapse_value_tt
			}
			set_global_flag = TRM_issuance_of_laws_for_local_humanity_flag
        }
	}
	TRM_ban_on_political_party_activities = {

	TRM_search_for_KPD = {
		icon = GFX_decision_infiltrate_state
		
		allowed = {
			original_tag = TRM
		}
		
		visible = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = TRN_country_collapse_decisions_end_flag }
			has_war_with = KPD
			NOT = { has_global_flag = TRM_search_for_KPD_flag }
		}
		custom_cost_text = decision_cost_ie_1000
		
		fire_only_once = yes

		custom_cost_trigger = {
			has_equipment = { infantry_equipment > 999 }
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 4000
		}

		complete_effect = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = infantry_equipment
				amount = -1000
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = enter_tt
			add_to_variable = {
				global.KPD_country_collapse_decisions_value = -5
				tooltip = KPD_country_collapse_value_tt
			}
			set_global_flag = TRM_search_for_KPD_flag
        }
	}

	TRM_commencement_of_anti_terrorism_operations_KPD = { # TRM = 가위 : KPD
		icon = GFX_decision_scissors
		
		targets = { KPD }

		allowed = {
			original_tag = TRM
		}
		
		visible = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = TRN_country_collapse_decisions_end_flag }
			has_war_with = KPD
			NOT = { has_global_flag = KPD_declaration_of_resistance_flag }
		}
		available = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = TRM_start_KPD_flag }
		}
		custom_cost_text = TRM_decision_cost_ie_150_pp_25
		
		fire_only_once = no

		custom_cost_trigger = {
			has_equipment = { infantry_equipment > 149 }
			has_political_power > 24
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 1000
			modifier = {
				has_country_flag = TRM_ai_guid_KPD_2_flag
				factor = 0.1
			}
			modifier = {
				has_country_flag = TRM_ai_guid_KPD_2_go_flag
				factor = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_country_flag = TRM_ai_guid_KPD_2_go_flag
				KPD = { is_ai = no }
				check_variable = { global.KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 4 }
				factor = 0.1
			}
			modifier = {
				KPD = { is_ai = no }
				check_variable = { global.KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 4 }
				factor = 0.7
			}
			modifier = {
				KPD = { is_ai = yes }
				check_variable = { global.KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 4 }
				factor = 8
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			set_variable = { global.TRM_KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 2 }
			set_variable = { TRM_anti_trinity_game_KPD_side_TRM_var = 2 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = infantry_equipment
				amount = -150
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				add_political_power = -25
			}
			set_country_flag = {
				flag = TRM_start_KPD_flag
				days = 45
				value = 1
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_active_mission = TRM_operation_outcome_KPD } }
				FROM = {
					activate_mission = TRM_impending_operation_KPD
				}
				activate_mission = TRM_operation_outcome_KPD
			}
			set_country_flag = {
				flag = TRM_ai_guid_KPD_2_flag
				days = 45
				value = 1
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				random_list = {
					1 = { set_country_flag = { flag = TRM_ai_guid_KPD_2_go_flag days = 100 value = 1 } }
					1 = { set_country_flag = { flag = TRM_ai_guid_KPD_3_go_flag days = 100 value = 1 } }
					1 = { set_country_flag = { flag = TRM_ai_guid_KPD_4_go_flag days = 100 value = 1 } }
				}
			}
        }
	}

	TRM_operation_outcome_KPD = { # 트리니티 측 완료용 타이머
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_prepare_civil_war
		
		allowed = {
			original_tag = TRM
		}

		activation = {
			always = no
		}

		is_good = no

		days_mission_timeout = 35

		available = {
			always = no
		}

		cancel_trigger = {
			has_global_flag = TRN_country_collapse_decisions_end_flag
		}

		timeout_effect = {
			set_variable = { TRM_anti_trinity_game_KPD_side_TRM_var = global.TRM_KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps }
			set_variable = { TRM_anti_trinity_game_KPD_side_KPD_var = global.KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps }
			hidden_effect = {
				if = { # 비김
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							global.TRM_KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = global.KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.12 days = 3 }
				}
				else_if = { # 가위 vs 바위 저항군 승
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							global.TRM_KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 2
						} 
						check_variable = {
							global.KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 3
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.13 days = 3 }
				}
				else_if = { # 가위 vs 보 트리니티 승
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							global.TRM_KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 2
						} 
						check_variable = {
							global.KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 4
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.11 days = 3 }
				}
				else_if = { # 바위 vs 가위 트리니티 승
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							global.TRM_KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 3
						} 
						check_variable = {
							global.KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 2
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.11 days = 3 }
				}
				else_if = { # 바위 vs 보 저항군 승
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							global.TRM_KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 3
						} 
						check_variable = {
							global.KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 4
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.13 days = 3 }
				}
				else_if = { # 보 vs 가위 저항군 승
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							global.TRM_KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 4
						} 
						check_variable = {
							global.KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 2
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.13 days = 3 }
				}
				else_if = { # 보 vs 바위 트리니티 승
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							global.TRM_KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 4
						} 
						check_variable = {
							global.KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 3
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.11 days = 3 }
				}
				else_if = { # 안내면 진거 트리니티
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							global.TRM_KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 0
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.13 days = 3 }
				}
				else_if = { # 안내면 진거 저항군
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							global.KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 0
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.11 days = 3 }
				}
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = no

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 4000
		}
	}

						check_variable = {
							global.TRM_KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 4
						}

							global.KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 3
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.11 days = 3 }
				}
				else_if = { # 안내면 진거 트리니티
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							global.TRM_KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 0
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.13 days = 3 }
				}
				else_if = { # 안내면 진거 저항군

							global.KPD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 0
						}

		}

		}

		available = {
			always = no
		}

			has_global_flag = TRN_country_collapse_decisions_end_flag
		}

		timeout_effect = {
			set_variable = { TRM_anti_trinity_game_NSD_side_TRM_var = global.TRM_NSD_country_collapse_decisions_rps }
			set_variable = { TRM_anti_trinity_game_NSD_side_NSD_var = global.NSD_country_collapse_decisions_rps }
			hidden_effect = {
				if = { # 비김
					limit = {

							global.TRM_NSD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = global.NSD_country_collapse_decisions_rps
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.21 days = 3 }
				}
				else_if = { # 가위 vs 바위 저항군 승
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							global.TRM_NSD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 2
						} 
						check_variable = {
							global.NSD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 3
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.23 days = 3 }
				}
				else_if = { # 가위 vs 보 트리니티 승
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							global.TRM_NSD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 2
						} 
						check_variable = {
							global.NSD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 4
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.21 days = 3 }
				}
				else_if = { # 바위 vs 가위 트리니티 승
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							global.TRM_NSD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 3
						} 
						check_variable = {
							global.NSD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 2
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.21 days = 3 }
				}
				else_if = { # 바위 vs 보 저항군 승
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							global.TRM_NSD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 3
						} 
						check_variable = {
							global.NSD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 4
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.23 days = 3 }
				}
				else_if = { # 보 vs 가위 저항군 승
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							global.TRM_NSD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 4
						} 
						check_variable = {
							global.NSD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 2
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.23 days = 3 }
				}
				else_if = { # 보 vs 바위 트리니티 승
					limit = {

							global.TRM_NSD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 4
						} 
						check_variable = {
							global.NSD_country_collapse_decisions_rps = 3
						}
					}
					country_event = { id = TRN_collapse_event.21 days = 3 }

				else_if = { # 안내면 진거 트리니티

	TRM_calm_storm = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_break_treaty
    		available = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						OR = {
							has_idea = BA_GER_defense
							has_idea = BA_GER_defense_week
							has_idea = BA_GER_defense_fail
						}
					}
				}
				any_country = {
					OR = {
						has_idea = BA_GER_defense
						has_idea = BA_GER_defense_week
						has_idea = BA_GER_defense_fail
					}
					OR = {
						exists = no
						NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
						has_capitulated = yes
						is_subject_of = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				event_target:TRM_collapse_event_target = {
					OR = {
						exists = no
						NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
						has_capitulated = yes
						is_subject_of = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
    		}
		
		activation = {
			always = no
		}
		visible = {
		}
		
		fire_only_once = yes
		is_good = no
		modifier = {
		}
		days_mission_timeout = TRM_calm_storm_days
		timeout_effect = {
			#진압 실패 - 반복적인 연쇄 붕괴 이벤트 체인 시작
			country_event = TRN_collapse_event.3
			activate_mission = TRM_calm_storm
			set_country_flag = TRM_calm_storm_flag
		}
		complete_effect = {
			add_ideas = TRM_collapse_3

			# 붕괴 이벤트가 진압 되었다면 TRM_calm_storm_days 변수값을 7을 깎습니다. 35를 초과하거나 7 미만으로 떨어지지 않습니다.
			add_to_variable = { TRM_calm_storm_days = -7 }
			clamp_variable = { var = TRM_calm_storm_days min = 7 max = 35 }
			
			#붕괴 이벤트가 진압되었으니 다시 국민정신을 제공하고 존재하는 모든 플래그를 제거합니다. (초기화)
			clr_global_flag = TRN_collapse_event_proceeding_flag

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_democratic_party }
				clr_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_democratic_party
			}
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_communism_party }
				clr_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_communism_party
			}
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_fascism_party }
				clr_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_fascism_party
			}
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_polish_party }
				clr_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_polish_party
			}
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_cathpalug }
				clr_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_cathpalug
			}
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_vigilante }
				clr_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_vigilante
			}
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_hifumi }
				clr_country_flag = TRN_collapse_event_hifumi
			}

			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = TRN_collapse_democratic_party_global }
				clr_global_flag = TRN_collapse_democratic_party_global
			}
			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = TRN_collapse_communism_party_global }
				clr_global_flag = TRN_collapse_communism_party_global
			}
			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = TRN_collapse_fascism_party_global }
				clr_global_flag = TRN_collapse_fascism_party_global
			}
			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = TRN_collapse_polish_party_global }
				clr_global_flag = TRN_collapse_polish_party_global
			}
			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = TRN_collapse_cathpalug_global }
				clr_global_flag = TRN_collapse_cathpalug_global
			}
			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = TRN_collapse_vigilante_global }
				clr_global_flag = TRN_collapse_vigilante_global
			}
			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = TRN_collapse_hifumi_global }
				clr_global_flag = TRN_collapse_hifumi_global
			}
		}

TRN_after_war_decisions = {

	TRM_Gehenna_Military_Civil_Administration = { # 군정 실시
		allowed = { always = no }
		icon = GFX_decision_SWE_set_army_budget

		visible = {
		}
		available = {
        	TRM = {
	            count_triggers = {
                	amount = 2
                	controls_state = 59
	                controls_state = 56
                	controls_state = 57
                	controls_state = 55
                	controls_state = 51
                	controls_state = 1052
                	controls_state = 1055
                	controls_state = 42
                	controls_state = 50
                	controls_state = 54
                	controls_state = 53
                	controls_state = 52
                	controls_state = 8
            	}
	        }
		}

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = { 
				AND = {
					BA_is_GER_core = yes
					NOT = { 
						is_core_of = TRM 
						state = 61
						state = 66
						state = 58
						state = 909
					}
				}
				controller = {
					tag = TRM
				}
			}
		}
		
		on_map_mode = decision_view_only

		days_remove = 14
		
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			command_power > 14.99
		}
		custom_cost_text = decision_cost_CP_15
		
		fire_only_once = yes

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 1000
		}
        modifier = { civilian_factory_use = 3 }
        complete_effect = {
			hidden_effect = { add_command_power = -15 }
        }
		
		remove_effect = {
			set_country_flag = TRM_complete_GMCA
			country_event = { id = TRN_after_war_event.20 } 
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = { 
					BA_is_GER_core = yes
					NOT = { 
						is_core_of = TRM 
						state = 61
						state = 66
						state = 58
						state = 909
					}
				}
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_Military_occupation }
			}
		}
	}

	TRM_GMCA_transfer = { # 군정에게 이양
		
		icon = GFX_decision_SWE_set_army_budget

			}
		}
		days_remove = 14

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 1000
		}

		cost = 5
		
		remove_effect = {

		}
	}

TRN_Mika_social_problem_cat = {
	TRN_Mika_social_problem = {
		priority = 999
		icon = GFX_decision_SWI_consolidate_council_power
		allowed = { tag = TRM } #manually activated
		selectable_mission = yes
		is_good = no
		fire_only_once = yes
		days_mission_timeout = TRN_Mika_social_problem_days
		fixed_random_seed = no
		cancel_trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_appeasement_germany_modifier }
			}
		}

		activation = {
			has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_appeasement_germany_modifier }
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 1000 }

		available = {
			has_country_flag = TRN_Mika_social_trinity_language_flag
			
			IF = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_completed_focus = TRN_unprovable_problem }
				}
				has_completed_focus = TRN_solving_social_problems
				NOT = {
					country_exists = GEM
					country_exists = GEH
				}
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			add_political_power = 50
			IF = {
				limit = { has_focus_tree = TRM_focus_tree }
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = ROOT
					popularity = 0.2
				}
			}
			IF = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_appeasement_germany_modifier }
				}
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_appeasement_germany_modifier }
			}
			IF = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_pro_german_propaganda_modifier }
				}
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_pro_german_propaganda_modifier }
			}
			IF = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_trinity_social_issues_modifier }
				}
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TRM_trinity_social_issues_modifier }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				IF = {
					limit = { 
						has_government = fascism
					}
					country_event = { id = trinity_flavor_events.31 days = 70 }
				}
			}
		}
		timeout_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { TRM_pro_german_propaganda_conscription_factor > -0.50 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { TRM_pro_german_propaganda_conscription_factor = TRM_monthly_conscription_factor_increase }
				clamp_variable = {
					var = TRM.TRM_pro_german_propaganda_conscription_factor
					min = -0.50
				}
				if = { #Apply penalties if CG is 100% or more
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = TRM_pro_german_propaganda_penalty_flag }
						check_variable = { var = TRM_pro_german_propaganda_conscription_factor value = -0.50 compare = less_than_or_equals }
					}
					set_country_flag = TRM_pro_german_propaganda_penalty_flag
					add_to_variable = { TRM_pro_german_propaganda_consumer_goods_expected_value = TRM_propaganda_expected_consumer_goods_penalty }

	## 짬통 디시전

	eden_treaty_negotiation_generic_propaganda = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_form_nation
		priority = 3

		allowed = {
			OR = {
				tag = TRM
				tag = GEH
				tag = GEM
			}
		}

		visible = {
			
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 20
		}

		days_remove = 21
		days_re_enable = 21

		cost = 15

		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { tag = TRM } # 트리니티
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = trn_nagisa_autocracy_flag } # 나기사 독재 키워드
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^0 = 5
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system = yes
				}
				else = {
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^0 = 10
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { tag = GEH } # 선도부
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = GEH_ako_route_focus } # 아코 루트 (민주주의)
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^0 = 10
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = GEH_main_route_focus } # 노멀 루트
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^0 = 10
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = GEH_hina_route_focus } # 히나 루트
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^0 = 5
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system = yes
				}
				else = { # 이오리 루트
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^0 = 5
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { tag = GEM } # 판데모니엄 소사이어티
				if = {
					limit = { 
						has_country_leader = {
							ruling_only = yes # default = yes
							character = GEH_dlfhgk
						}
					} # 이로하 지도자
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^1 = 15
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { 
						has_country_leader = {
							ruling_only = yes # default = yes
							character = GEH_dlqnzl
						}
					} # 이부키 지도자
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^1 = 15
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { 
						has_country_leader = {
							ruling_only = yes # default = yes
							character = GEH_motomiya_chiaki
						}
					} # 치아키 지도자
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^1 = 15
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { 
						has_country_leader = {
							ruling_only = yes # default = yes
							character = GEH_satsuki
						}
					} # 사츠키 지도자
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^1 = 10
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system = yes
				}
				else = { # 나치 집권 or 마코토
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^1 = 5
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system = yes
				}
			}
		}
		
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = tooltip_re_enable
				TIME = "21"
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = enter_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = eden_treaty_negotiation_generic_propaganda_tt
		}
	}
}

	## 짬통 디시전

	eden_treaty_negotiation_generic_propaganda_ALT = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_form_nation
		priority = 3

		allowed = {
			OR = {
				tag = TRM
				tag = GEH
				tag = GEM
			}
		}

		visible = {
			
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 20
		}

		days_remove = 21
		days_re_enable = 21

		cost = 15

		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { tag = TRM } # 트리니티
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = trn_nagisa_autocracy_flag } # 나기사 독재 키워드
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^0 = 5
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system_ALT = yes
				}
				else = {
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^0 = 10
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system_ALT = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { tag = GEH } # 선도부
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = GEH_ako_route_focus } # 아코 루트 (민주주의)
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^0 = 10
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = GEH_main_route_focus } # 노멀 루트
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^0 = 10
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = GEH_hina_route_focus } # 히나 루트
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^0 = 5
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system = yes
				}
				else = { # 이오리 루트
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^0 = 5
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { tag = GEM } # 판데모니엄 소사이어티
				if = {
					limit = { 
						has_country_leader = {
							ruling_only = yes # default = yes
							character = GEH_dlfhgk
						}
					} # 이로하 지도자
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^1 = 15
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system_ALT = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { 
						has_country_leader = {
							ruling_only = yes # default = yes
							character = GEH_dlqnzl
						}
					} # 이부키 지도자
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^1 = 15
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system_ALT = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { 
						has_country_leader = {
							ruling_only = yes # default = yes
							character = GEH_motomiya_chiaki
						}
					} # 치아키 지도자
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^1 = 15
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system_ALT = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { 
						has_country_leader = {
							ruling_only = yes # default = yes
							character = GEH_satsuki
						}
					} # 사츠키 지도자
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^1 = 10
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system_ALT = yes
				}
				else = { # 나치 집권 or 마코토
					add_to_variable = {
						global.eden_point_array^1 = 5
						tooltip = eden_point_array_tt
					}
					eden_treaty_negotiation_winner_check_system_ALT = yes
				}
			}
		}
		
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = tooltip_re_enable
				TIME = "21"
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = enter_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = eden_treaty_negotiation_generic_propaganda_tt
		}
	}

		}

		visible = {

		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = TRM_nagisa_constitution_reflection_on_past_history_trigger_tt
				OR = {
					has_war_with = GEI
					has_completed_focus = TRN_two_church_unity
				}
			}
			check_variable = { TRM_nagisa_constitution_in_progress_var = 0 }
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 20
		}

		cost = 30
		days_remove = 56

		fire_only_once = yes

		complete_effect = {
			set_variable = {
				TRM_nagisa_constitution_in_progress_var = 1
			}
		}

		remove_effect = {
			set_variable = {
				TRM_nagisa_constitution_in_progress_var = 0
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = TRM_nagisa_constitution_modifier_change
			add_to_variable = {
				TRM_nagisa_constitution_army_org_factor = 0.05
				tooltip = army_org_factor_tt
			}
			set_country_flag = TRM_nagisa_constitution_reflection_on_past_history_flag
		}
	}
}
TRM_mika_misono_problem_cat = {
	TRM_add_misono_sns_main_debug = {
		visible = {
			is_debug = yes
		}
		complete_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { TRM_mika_misono_sns_main_var < 11 }
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					TRM_mika_misono_sns_main_var = 1
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = {
					TRM_mika_misono_sns_main_var = 0
				}
			}
		}
	}

		}

		available = {
			hidden_trigger = { always = no }
		}

		is_good = no

		cancel_trigger = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = TRM_misono_mission_demands_military_factories_tt
				num_of_military_factories > TRM_desired_military_factories 
			}
		}

		cancel_effect = { # 성공
			army_experience = 10

			TRM_pater_demand_finalize_mission_effect = yes
			TRM_pater_demand_add_to_successful_missions = yes
			TRM_nagisa_bop_medium_increase_effect = yes
		}

		timeout_effect = { # 실패

				id = trinity_events_nagisa_misono_mission.100
				hours = 1
			}
			TRM_nagisa_bop_medium_decrease_effect = yes
		}
	}

	TRM_misono_mission_demands_conscription_law_change = {

		priority = 1

		icon = GFX_decision_JAP_naval_faction

		allowed = { always = no }

		visible = {
		}

		available = {
			hidden_trigger = { always = no }
		}

		days_mission_timeout = 90

		is_good = no

		cancel_trigger = {
			IF = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = TRM_in_on_disarmed_nation_flag 
				}
				OR = {
					has_idea = volunteer_only
					has_idea = limited_conscription
					has_idea = extensive_conscription
					has_idea = service_by_requirement
					has_idea = all_adults_serve
					has_idea = scraping_the_barrel
				}
			}
			ELSE_IF = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = TRM_in_on_volunteer_only_flag
				}
				OR = {
					has_idea = limited_conscription
					has_idea = extensive_conscription
					has_idea = service_by_requirement
					has_idea = all_adults_serve
					has_idea = scraping_the_barrel
				}
			}
			ELSE_IF = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = TRM_in_on_limited_conscription_flag
				}
				OR = {
					has_idea = extensive_conscription
					has_idea = service_by_requirement
					has_idea = all_adults_serve
					has_idea = scraping_the_barrel
				}
			}
			ELSE_IF = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = TRM_in_on_extensive_conscription_flag
				}
				OR = {
					has_idea = service_by_requirement
					has_idea = all_adults_serve
					has_idea = scraping_the_barrel
				}
			}
			ELSE_IF = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = TRM_in_on_service_by_requirement_flag
				}
				OR = {
					has_idea = all_adults_serve
					has_idea = scraping_the_barrel
				}
			}
			ELSE_IF = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = TRM_in_on_all_adults_serve_flag
				}
				has_idea = scraping_the_barrel
			}
		}

		cancel_effect = {

			add_command_power = 10
			navy_experience = 10

			clr_country_flag = TRM_misono_mission_demands_conscription_law_change_flag
			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt

			TRM_pater_demand_finalize_mission_effect = yes
			TRM_pater_demand_add_to_successful_missions = yes
			TRM_nagisa_bop_medium_increase_effect = yes
			
			hidden_effect = {
				IF = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = TRM_in_on_disarmed_nation_flag
					}
					clr_country_flag = TRM_in_on_disarmed_nation_flag
				}
				ELSE_IF = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = TRM_in_on_volunteer_only_flag
					}
					clr_country_flag = TRM_in_on_volunteer_only_flag
				}
				ELSE_IF = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = TRM_in_on_limited_conscription_flag
					}
					clr_country_flag = TRM_in_on_limited_conscription_flag
				}
				ELSE_IF = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = TRM_in_on_extensive_conscription_flag
					}
					clr_country_flag = TRM_in_on_extensive_conscription_flag
				}
				ELSE_IF = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = TRM_in_on_service_by_requirement_flag
					}
					clr_country_flag = TRM_in_on_service_by_requirement_flag
				}
				ELSE_IF = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = TRM_in_on_all_adults_serve_flag
					}
					clr_country_flag = TRM_in_on_all_adults_serve_flag
				}
			}
		}

		timeout_effect = { # 실패
			clr_country_flag = TRM_misono_mission_demands_conscription_law_change_flag
			country_event = {
				id = trinity_events_nagisa_misono_mission.100
				hours = 1
			}
			TRM_nagisa_bop_medium_decrease_effect = yes
		}
	}
